# Third-party notices

JasperEditor ships the following third-party JavaScript packages in its web application and in its
Remotion composition bundle (the production dependency closure of `apps/web` and `packages/compositions`,
the build tools whose output ships in them: CSS, bundler runtimes and entry code — and the font files the
engine serves).
Native components (FFmpeg, Remotion binaries, models) are listed in Help ▸ About ▸ Licenses.

Generated by `pnpm notices` (scripts/third-party-notices.mjs); do not edit. `pnpm notices --check` verifies it.

12 ported source records (code and algorithms ported from other projects, `licenses/ported/`) follow the packages.

Source: https://github.com/remotion-dev/remotion/tree/main/packages/bundler

### LICENSE.md

```text
# Remotion License

In Remotion 5.0, the license will slightly change. [View the changes here](https://github.com/remotion-dev/remotion/pull/3750).

---

Depending on the type of your legal entity, you are granted permission to use Remotion for your project. Individuals and small companies are allowed to use Remotion to create videos for free (even commercial), while a company license is required for for-profit organizations of a certain size. This two-tier system was designed to ensure funding for this project while still allowing the source code to be available and the program to be free for most. Read below for the exact terms of use.

- [Free License](#free-license)
- [Company License](#company-license)

## Free License

Copyright © 2026 [Remotion](https://www.remotion.dev)

### Eligibility

You are eligible to use Remotion for free if you are:

- an individual
- a for-profit organization with up to 3 employees
- a non-profit or not-for-profit organization
- evaluating whether Remotion is a good fit, and are not yet using it in a commercial way

### Allowed use cases

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person eligible for the "Free License", to use the software non-commercially or commercially for the purpose of creating videos and images and to modify the software to their own liking, for the purpose of fulfilling their custom use case or to contribute bug fixes or improvements back to Remotion.

### Disallowed use cases

It is not allowed to copy or modify Remotion code for the purpose of selling, renting, licensing, relicensing, or sublicensing your own derivate of Remotion.

### Warranty notice

The software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind, express or implied, including but not limited to the warranties of merchantability, fitness for a particular purpose, and non-infringement. In no event shall the author or copyright holders be liable for any claim, damages or other liability, whether in an action of contract, tort or otherwise, arising from, out of or in connection with the software or the use or other dealings in the software.

### Support

Support is provided on a best-we-can-do basis via GitHub Issues and Discord.

## Company License

You are required to obtain a Company License to use Remotion if you are not within the group of entities eligible for a Free License. This license will enable you to use Remotion for the allowed use cases specified in the Free License, and give you access to prioritized support (read the [Support Policy](https://www.remotion.dev/docs/support)).

Visit [remotion.pro](https://www.remotion.pro/license) for pricing and to buy a license.

### FAQs

Are you not sure whether you need a Company License because of an edge case? Here are some [frequently asked questions](https://www.remotion.pro/faq).
```

### LICENSE.md

```text
# Remotion License

In Remotion 5.0, the license will slightly change. [View the changes here](https://github.com/remotion-dev/remotion/pull/3750).

---

Depending on the type of your legal entity, you are granted permission to use Remotion for your project. Individuals and small companies are allowed to use Remotion to create videos for free (even commercial), while a company license is required for for-profit organizations of a certain size. This two-tier system was designed to ensure funding for this project while still allowing the source code to be available and the program to be free for most. Read below for the exact terms of use.

- [Free License](#free-license)
- [Company License](#company-license)

## Free License

Copyright © 2026 [Remotion](https://www.remotion.dev)

### Eligibility

You are eligible to use Remotion for free if you are:

- an individual
- a for-profit organization with up to 3 employees
- a non-profit or not-for-profit organization
- evaluating whether Remotion is a good fit, and are not yet using it in a commercial way

### Allowed use cases

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person eligible for the "Free License", to use the software non-commercially or commercially for the purpose of creating videos and images and to modify the software to their own liking, for the purpose of fulfilling their custom use case or to contribute bug fixes or improvements back to Remotion.

### Disallowed use cases

It is not allowed to copy or modify Remotion code for the purpose of selling, renting, licensing, relicensing, or sublicensing your own derivate of Remotion.

### Warranty notice

The software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind, express or implied, including but not limited to the warranties of merchantability, fitness for a particular purpose, and non-infringement. In no event shall the author or copyright holders be liable for any claim, damages or other liability, whether in an action of contract, tort or otherwise, arising from, out of or in connection with the software or the use or other dealings in the software.

### Support

Support is provided on a best-we-can-do basis via GitHub Issues and Discord.

## Company License

You are required to obtain a Company License to use Remotion if you are not within the group of entities eligible for a Free License. This license will enable you to use Remotion for the allowed use cases specified in the Free License, and give you access to prioritized support (read the [Support Policy](https://www.remotion.dev/docs/support)).

Visit [remotion.pro](https://www.remotion.pro/license) for pricing and to buy a license.

### FAQs

Are you not sure whether you need a Company License because of an edge case? Here are some [frequently asked questions](https://www.remotion.pro/faq).
```

### LICENSE.md

```text
# Remotion License

In Remotion 5.0, the license will slightly change. [View the changes here](https://github.com/remotion-dev/remotion/pull/3750).

---

Depending on the type of your legal entity, you are granted permission to use Remotion for your project. Individuals and small companies are allowed to use Remotion to create videos for free (even commercial), while a company license is required for for-profit organizations of a certain size. This two-tier system was designed to ensure funding for this project while still allowing the source code to be available and the program to be free for most. Read below for the exact terms of use.

- [Free License](#free-license)
- [Company License](#company-license)

## Free License

Copyright © 2026 [Remotion](https://www.remotion.dev)

### Eligibility

You are eligible to use Remotion for free if you are:

- an individual
- a for-profit organization with up to 3 employees
- a non-profit or not-for-profit organization
- evaluating whether Remotion is a good fit, and are not yet using it in a commercial way

### Allowed use cases

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person eligible for the "Free License", to use the software non-commercially or commercially for the purpose of creating videos and images and to modify the software to their own liking, for the purpose of fulfilling their custom use case or to contribute bug fixes or improvements back to Remotion.

### Disallowed use cases

It is not allowed to copy or modify Remotion code for the purpose of selling, renting, licensing, relicensing, or sublicensing your own derivate of Remotion.

### Warranty notice

The software is provided "as is", without warranty of any kind, express or implied, including but not limited to the warranties of merchantability, fitness for a particular purpose, and non-infringement. In no event shall the author or copyright holders be liable for any claim, damages or other liability, whether in an action of contract, tort or otherwise, arising from, out of or in connection with the software or the use or other dealings in the software.

### Support

Support is provided on a best-we-can-do basis via GitHub Issues and Discord.

## Company License

You are required to obtain a Company License to use Remotion if you are not within the group of entities eligible for a Free License. This license will enable you to use Remotion for the allowed use cases specified in the Free License, and give you access to prioritized support (read the [Support Policy](https://www.remotion.dev/docs/support)).

Visit [remotion.pro](https://www.remotion.pro/license) for pricing and to buy a license.

### FAQs

Are you not sure whether you need a Company License because of an edge case? Here are some [frequently asked questions](https://www.remotion.pro/faq).
```

# Ported source code

JasperEditor contains code and algorithms ported from these open-source projects (records in `licenses/ported/`,
licence texts below).

| Record | Upstream | Licence | Copyright |
|---|---|---|---|
| Code ported by the `audio-fx` workstream (audio effects, Essential Sound, Music Remix) | https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors |
| Code ported by the `audio-mix` workstream (loudness, true peak, mixer, ducking, voice-over) | https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors |
| Ported code: caption files, transcripts and text-based editing (wp `captions`) | https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors |
| Code ported by the `edit-structure` workstream | https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors |
| After Effects-style expressions (object model, array maths, controls) | https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors |
| Blend-mode formulas, Dissolve noise, Bezier ease inverse, roving and Auto Bezier rules (keyframes-blend) | https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors |
| Ported code: masks and track mattes (wp `masks`) | https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors; Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors |
| Agent-interface QA harness, UI automation audit and CLI conventions (from EffectCraft) | https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors |
| MCP conventions: strict arguments, annotations, export progress and cancellation (from FilmCraft) | https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors |
| Contributor rules, project-file and crash-recovery docs, layering and asset checks (process workstream) | https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors; Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors |
| Ported code: Whisper decoding and word-timestamp algorithms (wp `speech`) | https://github.com/openai/whisper | MIT | Copyright (c) 2022 OpenAI |
| Ported code: speech-to-text front end, word timing, VAD and speaker labelling (wp `speech`) | https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors |

## Code ported by the `audio-fx` workstream (audio effects, Essential Sound, Music Remix)

Record: `licenses/ported/audio-fx.md`

- **Upstream:** https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft (commit `771b614ad4c3fbefda5d668ce4384504b8c13f6d`)
- **Licence:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors
- **Ported by:** audio-fx workstream, 2026-10-07

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/lib.rs` (`AudioEffect`, dB helpers) | `packages/audio-fx/src/core/effect.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/core/math.ts` | The processor contract (planar in-place blocks, latency, reset) and numeric helpers |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/fft.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/core/fft.ts` | Radix-2 complex FFT (f64 here) |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/design.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/core/design.ts` | Bessel / Butterworth / Chebyshev I / elliptic prototypes → bilinear biquad cascades (Scientific Filter) |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/util.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/core/util.ts` | Delay lines, LFOs, one-pole / DC-block / all-pass, envelope follower, PRNG |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/stft.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/core/stft.ts` | Streaming √Hann STFT overlap-add |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/mod.rs` (registry and its tests) | `packages/audio-fx/src/registry.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/test/registry.test.ts` | Effect id → processor; the generic contract tests (block-size independence, extremes, reset) |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/eq.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/filter-eq/bank.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/filter-eq/eq.ts`, `packages/project/src/registry/audio/filter-eq.ts` | Band bank with glides, Parametric EQ, Simple Parametric / 3-band EQ, simple pass and shelving filters |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/filters.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/filter-eq/eq.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/filter-eq/fft-filter.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/filter-eq/scientific.ts` | Graphic EQ (10/20/30 bands), Notch Filter, FFT Filter, Scientific Filter |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/dynamics.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/dynamics/curves.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/dynamics/single.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/dynamics/limiter.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/dynamics/rack.ts` | Soft-knee static curves, Single-band Compressor, Expander/Gate, Hard Limiter (sample-peak engine), Dynamics rack |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/multiband.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/dynamics/rack.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/dynamics/single.ts` | Multiband Compressor (LR4 crossovers), Tube-modeled Compressor |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/restoration.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/restoration/dehum.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/restoration/spectral.ts` | DeHummer, DeNoise (adaptive / learned noise print) |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/declick.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/restoration/declick.ts` | Automatic Click Remover (LPC residual detection, AR interpolation) |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/essential.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/restoration/spectral.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/restoration/speech.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/stereo-channel.ts` | DeReverb, DeEsser, Enhance Speech, Stereo Width |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/time.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/delay-modulation.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/reverb/fdn.ts` | Delay (tempo sync), Analog Delay, Multitap Delay; the FDN Studio Reverb |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/modulation.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/delay-modulation.ts` | Chorus/Flanger, Flanger, Phaser |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/reverbs.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/reverb/convolution.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/reverb/surround.ts` | Partitioned convolution with procedural impulses (energy-compensated decay is ours), Surround Reverb |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/pitch.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/pitch.ts` | Phase-vocoder Pitch Shifter with identity phase locking |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/special.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/special/shaping.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/special/mastering.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/stereo-channel.ts` | Distortion, Guitar Suite, Mastering, Vocal Enhancer, Loudness Meter, Mute, Channel Mixer, Stereo Expander, Binauralizer, Ambisonics panner |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/channel.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/effects/stereo-channel.ts`, `packages/project/src/registry/audio/stereo-channel.ts` | Amplify, Channel Volume, Balance, Invert, Swap / Fill Left / Fill Right |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/effects/premiere_tests.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/test/filter-eq.test.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/test/stereo-channel.test.ts` | Premiere-behaviour tests (extended with RBJ / analytic expectations) |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/remix.rs` | `packages/audio-fx/src/analysis/remix.ts`, `packages/audio-fx/test/remix.test.ts` | Beat tracking, segment similarity graph, remix planning and rendering (the steady-tone guard is ours) |
| `crates/render/src/remix.rs`, `crates/engine/src/remix.rs` | `apps/engine/src/audio/remix.ts`, `apps/engine/src/routes/audio-fx.ts`, `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/essential.ts` | Engine-side planning of a clip range; the remix edit (here: grouped pieces with constant-power crossfades instead of one remixed clip) |
| `crates/project/src/essential.rs` | `packages/project/src/essential/model.ts`, `packages/project/src/essential/reconcile.ts` | Essential Sound types, sections, presets and their mapping to ordinary keyframable clip effects (`essential::apply`) |
| `crates/engine/src/essential_sound.rs` | `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/essential.ts`, `apps/web/src/audio-fx/ops.ts` | Essential Sound commands, Auto-Match, ducking triggers |
| `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/essential_sound.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/remix.rs` | `apps/web/src/panels/essentialSound/EssentialSoundPanel.tsx`, `apps/web/src/panels/timeline/state/controller.ts` | Used as a layout reference: type buttons, sections, presets, the Remix tool's drag feedback |

Notes: everything is TypeScript (f32 audio, f64 state where the reference used f64). Shared primitives come from
`@jasper/audio-dsp` (audio-mix's ports: RBJ biquad designs, smoothing, the true-peak interpolator and limiter, the
BS.1770 meter). New here: the AudioWorklet host (`packages/compositions/src/audio/fx-processor.ts`) and the offline
mixer host (`packages/audio-fx/src/host.ts`), Mono Maker, Loudness Auto Gain, the Effect Controls editors
(`apps/web/src/audio-fx/editors.tsx`), the MCP tools and the convolution decay energy compensation.

## Code ported by the `audio-mix` workstream (loudness, true peak, mixer, ducking, voice-over)

Record: `licenses/ported/audio-mix.md`

- **Upstream:** https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft
- **Licence:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors
- **Ported by:** audio-mix workstream, 2026-10-07

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/loudness.rs` | `packages/audio-dsp/src/loudness.ts` | BS.1770-4 / EBU R128 meter: momentary, short-term, integrated (histogram two-stage gate), LRA (Tech 3342), sample / true peak, normalisation gain |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/loudness.rs` (`k_prefilter`, `k_rlb`) | `packages/audio-dsp/src/kweight.ts` | K-weighting from the analog prototypes via the bilinear transform (any sample rate) |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/oversample.rs` | `packages/audio-dsp/src/oversample.ts` | Polyphase true-peak interpolator (kernel changed to libswresample's upsampling kernel: 32 taps, Kaiser β 9, cut-off at Nyquist) |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/biquad.rs` | `packages/audio-dsp/src/biquad.ts` | Biquads (TDF-II), RBJ designs, magnitude response |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/smooth.rs` | `packages/audio-dsp/src/smooth.ts` | Per-sample parameter smoothing |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/channels.rs` | `packages/audio-dsp/src/channels.ts`, `packages/project/src/audio/mixer.ts`, `packages/compositions/src/audio/graph.ts` | Channel layouts, ITU-R BS.775 up/downmix and 5.1 mixdown types, −3 dB pan law, balance, the 5.1 panner |
| `crates/audio-dsp/src/ducking.rs` | `packages/audio-dsp/src/ducking.ts`, `packages/project/src/audio/ducking.ts` | RMS envelope via prefix sums, activity detection with gap bridging and minimum length, sensitivity threshold, duck keyframes merged and clamped to the clip |
| `crates/audio-dsp/tests/loudness_oracle.rs` | `packages/audio-dsp/test/loudness-oracle.test.ts`, `packages/audio-dsp/test/oracle.ts` | Oracle tests against ffmpeg `ebur128` (extended with EBU Tech 3341/3342 cases, 44.1/48/96 kHz, 5.1) |
| `crates/project/src/mixer.rs` | `packages/project/src/schema/audio.ts`, `packages/project/src/audio/strip.ts` | Mixer strips, automation modes and lanes, sends, submix routing, input maps, gesture thinning (`thin_points`) and lane writing (`write_lane`) |
| `crates/render/src/mixer.rs` | `packages/project/src/audio/plan.ts`, `packages/project/src/audio/mixer.ts`, `packages/compositions/src/audio/graph.ts` | Mixer graph signal path, routing-aware solo, pre/post-fader sends and inserts, latency compensation |
| `crates/engine/src/mixer.rs` | `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/audio.ts`, `apps/web/src/audio/automation.ts` | Mixer commands, the automation recorder (Off/Read/Latch/Touch/Write, automatch), Audio Clip Mixer writes, Audio Gain |
| `crates/engine/src/voiceover.rs` | `apps/web/src/audio/voiceover.ts` | Voice-over record point / pre-roll / punch-in, countdown cue times and tone, file naming |
| `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/meters.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/mixer.rs` | `apps/web/src/panels/audioMeters/`, `apps/web/src/panels/audioMixer/` | Meter layout (dB scale, peak hold, loudness readout) and mixer strip layout |

Notes: everything is TypeScript. The true-peak kernel, the look-ahead true-peak limiter, the level meter, the WAV
codec and the windowed-sinc resampler are new. The offline mixer evaluates every lane per sample in the browser and in
Node; the preview runs the same matrices in a Web Audio graph.

## Ported code: caption files, transcripts and text-based editing (wp `captions`)

Record: `licenses/ported/captions.md`

- **Upstream:** https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft
- **Licence:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors
- **Ported by:** captions workstream, 2026-10-07

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/captions/src/lib.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/core.ts`, `packages/project/src/captions/formats/index.ts` | Cue / document model, `snap_to_frames`, `normalize`, format detection, text decoding (BOM, UTF-16, Windows-1252), clock times |
| `crates/captions/src/srt.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/srt.ts` | Forgiving SubRip reader and writer |
| `crates/captions/src/vtt.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/vtt.ts` | WebVTT reader/writer: cue ids, settings, `<v>` voices, STYLE/REGION/NOTE blocks |
| `crates/captions/src/cea608.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/cea608.ts` | CEA-608 character sets, parity, PACs, control codes |
| `crates/captions/src/scc.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/scc.ts` | Scenarist SCC: channel-1 608 decoder (pop-on, roll-up, paint-on), pop-on scheduler and writer |
| `crates/captions/src/mcc.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/mcc.ts` | MacCaption MCC: hex abbreviations, SMPTE ST 334-2 CDPs, CEA-708 service 1 writer and decoder |
| `crates/captions/src/stl.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/stl.ts` | EBU STL (Tech 3264): GSI/TTI blocks, ISO/IEC 6937, extension blocks |
| `crates/captions/src/ttml.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/ttml.ts`, `packages/project/src/captions/formats/xml.ts` | TTML / IMSC1 / DFXP reader and writer (roxmltree replaced by a small total XML reader) |
| `crates/captions/src/burn.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/model.ts` (`styleFromVttSettings`), `packages/compositions/src/layers/CaptionsLayer.tsx` | WebVTT `line:`/`align:` placement overrides, caption layout rules (frame-relative size, box, outline, anchor) |
| `crates/captions/tests/roundtrip.rs` | `packages/project/test/caption-formats.test.ts` | Round-trip property tests and reader totality tests (proptest → fast-check) |
| `crates/project/src/caption.rs` (`plain_text`) | `packages/project/src/captions/formats/core.ts` (`plainCaptionText`) | Markup stripping |
| `crates/project/src/transcript.rs` (`normalize_word`) | `packages/project/src/captions/words.ts` (`normalizeWord`) | Word normalisation for search and fillers |
| `crates/edit/src/transcript.rs` | `packages/project/src/captions/sequence-transcript.ts` | Sequence transcript, word at, paragraphs, search, word ranges, pauses, fillers (longest phrase first), filler ranges, merge ranges, caption blocks |
| `crates/edit/src/transcript/tests.rs`, `crates/edit/tests/fixtures/interview.transcript.json` | `packages/project/test/sequence-transcript.test.ts`, `packages/project/test/fixtures/interview.transcript.json` | Deterministic transcript tests and the interview fixture (times converted to seconds) |
| `crates/edit/src/captions.rs` | `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/captions.ts`, `packages/project/test/captions-commands.test.ts` | Caption add (clamped to the next caption), split, merge, set times, trim/move/delete semantics and their tests |

Notes: Rust → TypeScript; times are flicks (705 600 000 / s) instead of ticks; frame arithmetic uses BigInt so it is
exact for every rational rate. In JasperEditor captions are clips on caption tracks that own their words (a
transcript each), so split partitions words by time instead of duplicating the text (the text is duplicated only
when no word boundary lies between the halves, as in FilmCraft). Speech recognition (`crates/speech`) was not ported.

## Code ported by the `edit-structure` workstream

Record: `licenses/ported/edit-structure.md`

Upstream: FilmCraft — https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft
Licence: MIT OR Apache-2.0
Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/edit/src/through.rs` (`is_through_edit`, `through_edits`, `join_through_edits`, `track_gaps`, `sequence_gaps`, `clip_item_to`) | `packages/project/src/derive/index.ts` (`sameSource`, `isThroughEdit`), `packages/project/src/structure/through.ts`, `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/structure.ts` (`joinThroughEdits`) | Through-edit detection and joining, gap lists and navigation, trimming a copy of a clip to a range (Make Subsequence) |
| `crates/engine/src/commands.rs` (`nest`, `clip.group`, `clip.ungroup`) | `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/structure.ts` (`nestHandler`, `clip.group`, `clip.ungroup`) | Nest: lowest track per kind, linked nest clips, nested sequence with the parent's settings and track layout |
| `crates/engine/src/sequence_tools.rs` (`make_subsequence`, `go_to_gap`, `join_through_edits`, `reverse_match_frame`, `timeline_time_of`) | `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/structure.ts` (`makeSubsequence`), `apps/web/src/commands/structure.ts` (gap navigation, reverse match frame) | Subsequence naming `<name>_Sub_NN`, transitions inside the range, gap navigation, reverse match frame |
| `crates/engine/src/clip_ops.rs` (`add_frame_hold`, `insert_frame_hold_segment`, `frame_hold_options`, `remove_unused`) | `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/structure.ts` (`addFrameHold`, `insertFrameHoldSegment`, `frameHoldOptions`, `removeUnused`) | Frame hold semantics (split + hold, 2 s segment, hold on In / Out / playhead / sequence time / source time, Hold Filters) |
| `crates/project/src/lib.rs` (`source_time_at`, `effect_time_at`, `moving_source_time_at`) | `packages/project/src/render/scene.ts` (`clipContentTime`, `clipPictureTime`) | Picture time vs. effect time under a frame hold (Hold Filters) |
| `crates/edit/src/multicam.rs` (`flatten_items`) | `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/structure.ts` (`unnestHandler`) | Flattening a nest clip into the clips it shows (outer effects appended) |
| `crates/media/src/generators.rs` (`bars`, `leader`, `audio_sample`) | `packages/project/src/structure/generators.ts` | HD colour bars layout, the counting leader sweep/rings, the 1 kHz tone and leader blips (extended: Premiere's leader colours and cue options, anti-aliasing, stroke numerals) |
| `crates/render/src/lib.rs` (`render_seq_tracks` adjustment branch, `adjustment_region`, depth guard) | `packages/compositions/src/layers/AdjustmentScope.tsx`, `apps/engine/src/export/native.ts` (adjustment branch of `drawStack`) | Adjustment layers: effects on the composite below, mixed back with opacity / blend, limited to a region |

## After Effects-style expressions (object model, array maths, controls)

Record: `licenses/ported/expressions.md`

- **Upstream:** https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft (commit `2fadd14`)
- **Licence:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors
- **Ported by:** expressions workstream, 2026-10-07

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/expr/src/prelude.js` | `packages/expressions/src/prelude.ts` | the AE expression object model and global functions (array maths helpers, vector maths, interpolation, random/gaussRandom/seedRandom, time and colour conversions, Comp/Layer/property group/Prop/Markers objects, loopIn/loopOut, wiggle, smooth, valueAtTime, key/nearestKey, text style getters), adapted to a synchronous host and a Premiere-style document |
| `crates/expr/src/rewrite.rs` | `packages/expressions/src/compile.ts` | the array-maths operator rewrite (`+ - * /` → helper calls, unary minus, compound assignments, nesting limit), re-implemented on an acorn AST; its test cases are in `packages/expressions/test/compile.test.ts` |
| `crates/expr/src/noise.rs` | `packages/expressions/src/prelude.ts` | value noise (1D/3D, quintic fade), lattice hashing and the octave wiggle sum, ported to 32-bit integer hashing |
| `crates/expr/src/host.rs`, `crates/expr/src/runtime.rs` | `packages/expressions/src/world.ts`, `packages/expressions/src/values.ts`, `packages/expressions/src/runtime.ts` | the host request set (comp/layer/child/property info, values, transforms, markers), name/alias matching of properties, result conversion rules and AE-style error messages |
| `crates/effects/src/controls.rs` | `packages/project/src/registry/expression-controls.ts` | the Expression Controls effects (Slider, Angle, Checkbox, Color, Point, 3D Point, Layer, Dropdown Menu) and their parameters |

## Blend-mode formulas, Dissolve noise, Bezier ease inverse, roving and Auto Bezier rules (keyframes-blend)

Record: `licenses/ported/keyframes-blend.md`

- **Upstream:** https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft (commit `2fadd14`)
- **Licence:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors
- **Ported by:** keyframes-blend, 2026-10-07

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/color/src/blend.rs` | `packages/project/src/blend-modes.ts` | `blend_pixel`, separable / non-separable formulas, HDR mode set → TypeScript (`makeBlendKernel`, `blendPixel`) |
| `crates/gpu/src/shaders/common.wgsl` | `packages/project/src/registry/fx/glsl/blend.ts` | `blend_pixel`, `hash_noise` → GLSL ES 3.00 (`fx_layerBlend`, `fx_blendNoise`) |
| `crates/raster/src/lib.rs` (`hash_noise`) | `packages/project/src/blend-modes.ts` | Dissolve's integer hash (`dissolveNoise`) |
| `crates/keyframe/src/lib.rs` | `packages/project/src/keyframes.ts` | closed-form inverse of the 100 % / 100 % ease time curve (`solve_u`); influences not normalised (`ease_progress`); roving run idea (`retime_roving`) |
| `crates/keyframe/src/lib.rs` | `packages/project/src/keyframes-spatial.ts` | proportional Auto Bezier spatial tangents (`auto_spatial`) |
| `crates/keyframe/src/lib.rs` (tests) | `packages/project/test/keyframes-ease-precision.test.ts` | live After Effects samples of overlapping influences used as test oracles |

Notes: the blend formulas are a straight port, plus an 8-bit-grid tie rule for Hard Mix and a luminance tie for
Darker / Lighter Color so GPU and CPU agree. The ease solver adds a double-double midpoint expansion and a
bisection-safeguarded Newton with a step criterion. Roving runs are evaluated as one eased segment (EffectCraft spaces
roving keys for constant speed only). Dancing Dissolve, the Classic modes and Luminescent Premul are not ported
(not in Premiere's menu).

## Ported code: masks and track mattes (wp `masks`)

Record: `licenses/ported/masks.md`

Licence of the ported code: **MIT OR Apache-2.0** (we use it under MIT).

## EffectCraft — https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft

Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors

| Upstream | Ours | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/path/src/interp.rs` | `packages/project/src/path/interp.ts` | Smart path interpolation: arc-length vertex insertion (de Casteljau), shape-context (Belongie–Malik–Puzicha) cyclic correspondence with winding fix, Procrustes in-betweens, crossing count |
| `crates/path/src/trace.rs` | `packages/project/src/path/trace.ts` | Auto-trace: marching squares with saddle resolution and padded borders, Douglas–Peucker on closed loops, nesting depth, per-span cubic fit with chord/smooth tangent blend |
| `crates/path/src/fit.rs` | `packages/project/src/path/trace.ts` (`fitCubicWithTangents`) | Least-squares cubic with fixed end tangents + Newton reparameterisation |
| `crates/path/src/feather.rs` | `packages/project/src/masks/raster.ts` (`featherProfileAt`, profile placement), `packages/project/src/masks/glsl.ts` (`profileAt`) | Variable-width feather: feather points on segments, outer/inner radii interpolated along the arc length with tension (smoothstep ↔ linear), wrap-around on closed paths |
| `crates/path/src/lib.rs` (`to_kurbo` vertex model) | `packages/project/src/path/model.ts` | Vertex / relative tangent model, segments, ellipse construction |
| `crates/render/src/masks.rs` (mode algebra) | `packages/project/src/masks/model.ts` (`combineMaskMode`) | Add / Subtract / Intersect / Lighten / Darken / Difference |
| `crates/track/src/mask.rs` | `apps/engine/src/tracking/mask.ts` | Mask tracking approach: features inside the outline, pyramidal LK with forward–backward check, robust model fit with fallback, outline moved by the step |

## FilmCraft — https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft

Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors

| Upstream | Ours | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/render/src/mask.rs`, `crates/gpu/src/mask.wgsl` | `packages/project/src/masks/raster.ts`, `packages/project/src/masks/glsl.ts` (WGSL → GLSL ES 3.00) | Signed-distance mask coverage: nonzero winding + distance to the flattened outline, `falloff(s, feather)` (analytic 1-px edge at feather 0, centred smooth ramp), opacity / inversion, first-mode start values (Subtract / Intersect / Darken start from 1) |
| `crates/project/src/mask.rs` | `packages/project/src/schema/mask.ts`, `packages/project/src/masks/model.ts` | Premiere mask model: Mask Path / Feather (default 10) / Opacity / Expansion, Inverted, mode, tracking method; "Mask (n)" naming |
| `crates/engine/src/masks.rs` | `packages/project/src/commands/handlers/mask.ts` | Command shapes (add / set / move vertex / add & remove vertex in every key / convert vertex / track writing path keys) |
| `crates/render/src/vfx/color.rs` (`track_matte`, `apply_matte`) | `packages/project/src/registry/fx/premiere-adjust-color-keying/keying.ts` (Track Matte Key) | Track matte: another track's composite sampled through the layer → output map, Matte Alpha / Matte Luma, Reverse |

Record: `licenses/ported/mcp-cli-filmcraft.md`

- **Licence:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors
- **Ported by:** mcp-cli workstream, 2026-10-07

|---|---|---|
| `crates/automation/src/server.rs` (`unknown_arg`, annotations test) | `apps/mcp/src/strict.ts`, `packages/project/src/agent/strict.ts`, `apps/mcp/test/conventions.test.ts` | unknown arguments name themselves and the accepted ones; every tool has a title and the four hints, checked by a test (extended here to nested objects, union members, typo suggestions and agent commands) |
| `crates/automation/src/long_job.rs` | `apps/mcp/src/context.ts` (`progressReporter`, `waitJob`, `cancelJobAndWait`), `apps/engine/src/export/service.ts` (`copyNoOverwrite`) | progress at most every 100 ms and strictly increasing; `notifications/cancelled` cancels the job and the partial output is deleted |
| `apps/filmcraft-cli/src/args.rs` | `apps/mcp/src/jasper-cli.ts` (`kvArgs`) | `key=value` arguments parsed as JSON when they parse, dotted keys nest; `run` prints one JSON line per command and stops at the first failure unless `--keep-going` |

outputs is replaced by the engine job queue's own partial registry (the exact files a job claimed).

- **Licence:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors; Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors
- **Ported by:** process workstream, 2026-10-07

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| effectcraft `AGENTS.md`, filmcraft `AGENTS.md` | `AGENTS.md` | structure and wording of the never-crash and asset rules, the attribution sidecar format (§1.3) |
| effectcraft `CLAUDE.md`, filmcraft `CLAUDE.md`, filmcraft `docs/agents.md` §6–7 | `CLAUDE.md` | session start, Definition of Done checklist, honest progress reporting, the "ask a human only about" list |
| effectcraft `ATTRIBUTION.md` | `ATTRIBUTION.md` | index layout |

| effectcraft `docs/gaps.md`, `docs/parity.md`; filmcraft `ROADMAP.md` | `docs/gaps.md`, `docs/parity.md` | structure: two numbers (breadth vs readiness), workstreams with "done when", the evidence rule |
| filmcraft `docs/project-files.md` | `docs/project-files.md` | structure and migration rules ("never edit a shipped migration", bump when an older build would drop data, the first-save schema backup) and the crash-recovery journal design (snapshot ~1 s after an edit, held under a lock, offered on the next start) |

Notes: documentation and design only; no Rust code was translated. The recovery journal here lives in the engine
and uses the editing lease as its lock instead of an OS file lock.

## Ported code: Whisper decoding and word-timestamp algorithms (wp `speech`)

Record: `licenses/ported/speech-openai-whisper.md`

- **Upstream:** https://github.com/openai/whisper
- **Licence:** MIT
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2022 OpenAI
- **Ported by:** speech workstream, 2026-10-07

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| `whisper/decoding.py` (`ApplyTimestampRules`, `SuppressBlank`, `SuppressTokens`, `BeamSearchDecoder`, `MaximumLikelihoodRanker`) | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/decode.ts`, `apps/engine/src/speech/worker/whisper.ts` (`decodeBeam`) | Logit filters and timestamp rules; beam search (patience 1) and ranking by mean log-probability |
| `whisper/transcribe.py` (`transcribe`, `decode_with_fallback`) | `apps/engine/src/speech/worker/whisper.ts`, `apps/engine/src/speech/core/decode.ts` (`parseSegments`, `compressionRatio`) | Seek loop, segment parsing, temperature fallback (compression ratio 2.4, log-probability −1, no-speech 0.6), conditioning on previous text, seek to the last word with word timestamps |
| `whisper/timing.py` (`find_alignment`, `merge_punctuations`, `add_word_timestamps`) | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/align.ts` | Word boundaries from DTW jumps, punctuation merging, median-duration capping, segment boundary corrections |
| `whisper/tokenizer.py` (`LANGUAGES`, `split_to_word_tokens`, `split_tokens_on_unicode`) | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/tokenizer.ts`, `apps/web/src/speech/model.ts` (`WHISPER_LANGUAGES`) | Word splitting (no-space languages), the language list |

Notes: Python/PyTorch → TypeScript on ONNX Runtime; no weights are included (models download on demand, see
`apps/engine/src/processing/models.json` `speech`). Hallucination-silence skipping and `clip_timestamps` were not ported.

## Ported code: speech-to-text front end, word timing, VAD and speaker labelling (wp `speech`)

Record: `licenses/ported/speech.md`

- **Upstream:** https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft
- **Licence:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors
- **Ported by:** speech workstream, 2026-10-07

| Upstream path | Our path | What |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/speech/src/mel.rs` | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/mel.ts` | Whisper log-mel front end (Slaney filterbank, reflect padding, framing, normalisation); the DFT became a mixed-radix FFT |
| `crates/speech/src/whisper/tokenizer.rs` | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/tokenizer.ts` | `tokenizer.json` byte-level BPE decoding, special tokens, language tokens, non-text specials |
| `crates/speech/src/whisper/align.rs` | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/align.ts` (`medianFilter`, `dtw`, `alignTokens`) | Cross-attention normalisation, median filter, DTW token alignment |
| `crates/speech/src/whisper/mod.rs` | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/decode.ts` (`applyFilters`, `repeating`), `apps/engine/src/speech/worker/whisper.ts` | Timestamp rules, suppression, no-speech skip, repetition guard, window/seek loop, language detection |
| `crates/speech/src/vad.rs` | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/vad.ts` (`frameDb`, `vadThreshold`, `tightenWords`) | Energy-based word-bound tightening (30 % floor→speech, ≥ 6 dB, 60 ms minimum) |
| `crates/speech/src/diarize.rs` | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/diarize.ts` | MFCC 1–19 mean/std chunk vectors, average-linkage clustering, small-cluster folding, numbering by first appearance |
| `crates/speech/src/models.rs` | `apps/engine/src/speech/models.ts` | Model store: pinned files, sha256 verification, `.part` downloads, installed/missing bytes |
| `crates/speech/src/lib.rs` (`word_error_rate`) | `apps/engine/src/speech/core/wer.ts` | Word error rate |
| `crates/speech/src/{vad,diarize,mel,lib}.rs` tests, `crates/speech/src/whisper/{align,mod}.rs` tests | `apps/engine/test/speech-core.test.ts` | Unit tests (synthetic voices, VAD fixture, DTW band, median filter, repetition guard, WER) |

Notes: Rust (candle) → TypeScript on ONNX Runtime (the onnx-community Whisper exports with cross-attention outputs
instead of safetensors); times are 16 kHz samples in the worker and flicks in the model. Additions over the
reference: English-only models, beam search, temperature fallback, conditioning on previous text, OpenAI's word
duration heuristics and punctuation merging (see `speech-openai-whisper.md`), voice-aware seek, word-bound repair at
pauses and growth into adjacent voice, an exact speaker-count hint, translation, sequence-mix transcription.
